	KOR_debug_toggle_all_decision_uis = {
		icon = generic_decision
		visible = {
			is_debug = yes
			tag = KOR
		}
		cost = 0
		priority = 200
		fire_only_once = no
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0
		}
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_debug_toggle_all_decision_uis_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_debug_show_all_decision_uis_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = KOR_debug_show_all_decision_uis_flag
				}
				else = {
					set_country_flag = KOR_debug_show_all_decision_uis_flag
				}
			}
		}
	}

				KOR_conservative_pop = -1
			}

			}

### TFR - 남북대립 디시전 ###
# 60일 카운트다운(kimminseok_korea_war.10) 중에만 준비 디시전 노출.
# 각 갈래 3단계 순차 진행, 완료 시 progress 변수 증가.

kimminseok_korea_war_confrontation = {
	
	KOR_kimminseok_ai_war_start = {
		icon = generic_industry
		priority = 90
		allowed = { original_tag = KOR }
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_gaw_kimminseok_trigger_flag
			is_ai = yes
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 0
		complete_effect = {
			country_event = kimminseok_korea_war.24
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 100 }
	}
	
	### User timeout
	
	KOR_kimminseok_war_start_timeout = {
		icon = generic_crossed_guns
		allowed = { tag = KOR }
		cancel_trigger = {
			hidden_trigger = {

KOR_defense_reform_category = {
	KOR_defense_reform_token_growth_month = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_martial_dec
		visible = {
			tag = KOR
			has_completed_focus = KOR_yoon_army_upper
		}
		activation = {
			has_completed_focus = KOR_yoon_army_upper
		}
		available = {
			always = no
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		selectable_mission = no
		is_good = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		priority = 15
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				activate_mission = KOR_defense_reform_token_growth_month
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_token_change = 1 }
      				TFRKP_defreform_add_token_effect = yes
				}
				50 = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_not_progress_dfreform_TT
				}
			}
		}
	}
	### 복무환경 개선 (포스터 1) ###

	TFRKP_KOR_defreform_welfare_01 = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_defense_reform_institutional_reform
		allowed = { tag = KOR }
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = TFRKP_show_defreform_GUI
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { KOR_defreform_focus_area = 1 }
				}
				is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = KOR_defreform_token_var
				value = 1
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		custom_cost_text = TFRKP_defreform_token_cost_1
		available = { always = yes }	# 필요 시 자체 중점/조건으로 교체
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 60
		cost = 0
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_token_change = -1 }
				TFRKP_defreform_add_token_effect = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_defreform_trust_add_5_TT
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_trust_change = 5 }
				TFRKP_defreform_add_trust_effect = yes
			}
			add_war_support = 0.01
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 5 }
	}

KOR_Yoon_inner_circle_category = {
	KOR_appoint_choo_kyung_ho_decision = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_youngsan
		available = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TFRKOR_yhg_cd_flag
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = TFRKOR_yhg_cd_days_tt
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = TFRKOR_yhg_cd_flag
					}
				}
			}
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = KOR_appoint_decision_available_tt
					CHARACTER = KOR_choo_kyung_ho
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 4
					}
					check_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 4
					}
					check_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 4
					}
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
		}
		cost = 150
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
		}
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = KOR_ascension_of_character_tt
				CHARACTER = KOR_choo_kyung_ho
			}
			show_ideas_tooltip = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ns
			if = {
				#Just to make it easy to debug stuff for development
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = {
							TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 4
						}
						check_variable = {
							TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 4
						}
						check_variable = {
							TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 4
						}
					}
				}
				log = "Advisor is already appointed"
			}
			else_if = {
				# 만약 윤핵관 자리가 꽉찼다면 - 누군가를 교체 해야합니다.
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 2
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 2
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 2
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				log = "Yoon inner circle slots are full"
				hidden_effect = {
					set_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor = 4
					}
					country_event = {
						id = koryhg.1
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = {
					localization_key = TFR_KOR_event_to_replace_ascended_advisor_tt
					EVENT = koryhg.1.t
					CHARACTER = KOR_choo_kyung_ho
				}
			}
			else = {
				log = "Advisor appointed to Yoon inner circle"
				hidden_effect = {
					add_ideas = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ns
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 1
							compare = less_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 4
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 1
							compare = less_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 4
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 1
							compare = less_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 4
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = {
					flag = TFRKOR_yhg_cd_flag
					days = 90
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
				command_power > 15
			}
			else = {
				command_power > 20
			}
		}
		visible = {
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 1157
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 20
		days_remove = 2
		complete_effect = {
			add_command_power = -10
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_prc_nuke_tt
		}
		remove_effect = {
			launch_nuke = {
				province = 10068
			}
			launch_nuke = {
				province = 13691
			}
			launch_nuke = {
				province = 7221
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 50
		}
	}

KOR_LJS_government = {
	LJS_decision_2028_election_event_countdown_mission = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_LJS_1
		fire_only_once = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		is_good = no
		days_mission_timeout = 339 # 2027.5.9 -> 2028.4.12
		priority = 1920
		visible = {
			tag = KOR
			has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_nothing_ppp_focus_1
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_election_2028_done_flag }
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				always = no
			}
		}
		activation = {
			has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_nothing_ppp_focus_1
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_election_2028_done_flag }
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = LJS_decision_2028_election_event_countdown_mission_tt
		}
	}
	LJS_constitution_deadline_mission = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_LJS_1
		fire_only_once = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		is_good = no
		days_mission_timeout = 365
		priority = 1918
		visible = {
			tag = KOR
			has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitution_deadline_active_flag
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_process_finished_flag }
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				always = no
			}
		}
		activation = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitution_deadline_active_flag
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_process_finished_flag }
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_constitution_deadline_timeout_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitution_deadline_active_flag
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_process_started_flag }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_process_finished_flag }
					}
					set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_deadline_failed_flag
					set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_not_attempted_flag
					set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_failed_flag
					set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_process_finished_flag
					country_event = { id = korea.1360 hours = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	LJS_montly_rating = {
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		visible = {
			always = yes
		}
		priority = 1919
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				always = no
			}
		}
		activation = {
			has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_nothing_ppp_focus_1
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = LJS_weekly_rating_tt
			set_temp_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp
				value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_post_1351_phase_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 199 }
					}
					multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 1.7 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 150 } }
					}
					multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 0.25 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_failed_flag
					}
					multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 1.3 }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_wartime_government_phase_flag
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 0.5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_flag
				}
				# 임기 후반의 선택형 보상은 긍정평가도의 0.4배로 산출한다.
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 0.4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_election_2028_done_flag }
				}
				# 취임부터 제23대 총선까지는 긍정평가도의 0.5배만큼 추진력을 얻는다.
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 0.5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 199 }
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 1.7 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 179 }
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 1.4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 150 }
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 1.1 }
			}
			else = {
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 0.25 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_LJS_jeong_cheong_rae_ending_locked = yes
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp = 0.5 }
			}
			round_temp_variable = KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp
			if = {
				limit = {
					date > 2031.12.31
					KOR_is_lee_PPP_path = yes
				}
				# 2032년부터는 월간 집계를 계속하되 추진력만 얻지 않는다.
				set_variable = { KOR_LJS_late_term_momentum_reward_var = 0 }
				clr_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_choice_pending_flag
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_flag
						date > 2029.6.28
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_post_1351_phase_flag }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_great_asia_war }
					KOR_is_lee_PPP_path = yes
				}
				# 2029년 6월 29일부터 대아대전 발발 전까지는 추진력과 군사 버프 중 하나를 선택한다.
				set_variable = {
					var = KOR_LJS_late_term_momentum_reward_var
					value = KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp
				}
				set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_choice_pending_flag
				country_event = { id = korea.1364 hours = 6 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = {
					var = KOR_LJS_momemtum_var
					value = KOR_LJS_momentum_gain_temp
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_wartime_government_phase_flag
				}
				KOR_LJS_pirodo_down_auto_wartime = yes
			}
			else = {
				KOR_LJS_pirodo_down_auto = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							AND = {
								KOR_LJS_jeong_cheong_rae_ending_locked = yes
								OR = {
									has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_wartime_monthly_approval_penalty_flag
									has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_broken_cooperation_monthly_approval_penalty_flag
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							KOR_LJS_jeong_cheong_rae_ending_locked = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
							value = 1.3
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_wartime_government_phase_flag
							date > 2031.5.31
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
							value = 1.7
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_wartime_government_phase_flag
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
							value = 0.9
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_post_1351_phase_flag
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
							value = 0.3
						}
					}
					else = {
						subtract_from_variable = {
							var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
							value = 0.5
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_wartime_monthly_approval_penalty_flag
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
						value = 2
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_broken_cooperation_monthly_approval_penalty_flag
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
						value = 1.5
					}
				}
			}
			KOR_LJS_pirodo_down = yes
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_pirodo_var_visible
				value = KOR_LJS_pirodo_var
			}
			KOR_LJS_liberal_down_auto = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				KOR_LJS_clamp_approval_rating_to_cap_effect = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_visible
				value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
			}
			KOR_LJS_rating_bad = yes
			KOR_LJS_government_add_value_to_line = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_government_enable_flag
					}
					activate_mission = LJS_montly_rating
				}
			}
		}
	}

					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_plp_party_head_change_start_flag }
					has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_flag
					has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ongoing_ph_flag
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_PLP_party_setup_flag } 
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_plp_party_head_change_start_flag
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_flag }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ongoing_ph_flag }
		}
		activation = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_plp_party_head_change_start_flag
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_flag }

		is_good = no
		days_mission_timeout = 180
		fire_only_once = no
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				activate_mission = KOR_plp_party_influence_auto_upgrade_timeout
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { PLPHeadFaction_var = 1 } }
				}
				random_list = {
					60 = {
						set_temp_variable = {
							plp_influence_party_temp = 1
						}
						set_temp_variable = {
							plp_influence_var_temp = 1
						}
						KOR_PLP_influence_add = yes
					}
					40 = {
						
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line 
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { PLPHeadFaction_var = 2 } }
				}
				random_list = {
					60 = {
						set_temp_variable = {
							plp_influence_party_temp = 2
						}
						set_temp_variable = {
							plp_influence_var_temp = 1
						}
						KOR_PLP_influence_add = yes
					}

	KOR_plp_kim_trait_upgrade = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_plp_kim_trait_upgrade
		cancel_trigger = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_PLP_party_setup_flag } 
			}
		}
		visible = {
			tag = KOR
			has_country_leader = {
				character = KOR_kim_jae_yeon_char
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits
				has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_2
				has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_3
				has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_4
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = KOR_plp_kim_trait_upgrade_trigger_tt
				check_variable = { KOR_PLP_president_influence_var > 2 }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = KOR_plp_kim_trait_upgrade_trigger_pp_tt
				check_variable = { KOR_plp_pp_inf_var > 1 }
			}
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_4
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 100
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1000
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits
				}
				KOR_kim_jae_yeon_char = {
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits
						add = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_2
						ideology = progressive_democracy
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_2
				}
				KOR_kim_jae_yeon_char = {
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_2
						add = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_3
						ideology = progressive_democracy
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader_with_trait = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_3
				}
				KOR_kim_jae_yeon_char = {
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_3
						add = kor_kim_jae_yeon_traits_4
						ideology = progressive_democracy
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_plp_kim_trait_upgrade_end_tt
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			set_temp_variable = {
				plp_influence_president_var_temp = -3
			}
			KOR_PLP_president_influence_add = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				plp_influence_party_temp = 1
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				plp_influence_var_temp = -2
			}
			KOR_PLP_influence_add = yes
		}
	}

				add_to_variable = { KOR_supreme_council_loyalty_var = 6 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 0 }
	}

		priority = 10
		fire_only_once = no
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_riot_suppression_selected_gwangju_flag
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_riot_suppression_gwangju_completed_flag }
			check_variable = { KOR_riot_resistance_gwangju_var > 0 }
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = KOR_riot_available_martial_law_troops_var
				value = 1
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		custom_cost_text = KOR_riot_martial_law_troop_cost_1
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_riot_resistance_suppression_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { KOR_riot_available_martial_law_troops_var = -1 }
				KOR_riot_suppress_gwangju_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	KOR_kfxx_fighter_project_mission = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_lee_kfxx_icon
		allowed = { tag = KOR }
		visible = { has_country_flag = KOR_kfxx_project_category_flag }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		priority = 1000
		days_mission_timeout = 1000
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_kfxx_project_complete_tt
			set_variable = { KOR_kfxx_project_days_var = 0 }
			set_technology = {
				modern_small_airframe = 1
			}
			air_experience = 50
			add_to_variable = { KOR_army_reform = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { military_development_var_temp = 0.03 }
			add_military_development = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_tech = improved_small_airframe
						}
					}
					set_technology = {
						improved_small_airframe = 1
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_tech = advanced_small_airframe
						}
					}
					set_technology = {
						advanced_small_airframe = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# [주요 개혁] 국방 개혁 기본계획 - 공세·방어 종합 강화
	TFRKP_KOR_kim_defwar_reform_01 = {
		icon = GFX_KOR_icarus_mil
		allowed = { tag = KOR }
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_lee_kim_min_seok_flag
			OR = {
				check_variable = { KOR_reform_tab = 2 }
				is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_vic_kim_10
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = TFRKP_icarus_wax_10_TT
				check_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax > 9 }
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 30
		cost = 30
		remove_effect = {
			TFRKP_kms_add_army_decision_effect = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_icarus_wax_subtract_10_TT
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_icarus_wax_change = -10 }
				TFRKP_icarus_add_wax_effect = yes
			}
			army_experience = 25
			add_to_variable = {
				var = KOR_roka_army_attack_factor_dynamic_var
				value = 0.01
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = KOR_roka_army_defence_factor_dynamic_var
				value = 0.01
			}
			effect_tooltip = {
				swap_ideas = {
					add_idea = KOR_roka_dynamic_dummy_military_idea2
					remove_idea = KOR_yoon_roka_dynamic_dummy_idea
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { 
			base = 30
		}
	}

	########################################
	### 경제 성장률 미션 (탭 무관, 상단 고정) ###
	########################################
	KOR_prepare_for_economy_reset = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_martial_dec
		allowed = {
			always = no
		}
		visible = {
			tag = KOR
			has_country_flag = KOR_allowed_kimminseok_economy_system_flag
		}
		available = { has_country_flag = KOR_asians_last_democratic_country_flag }
		fire_only_once = no
		selectable_mission = no
		is_good = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 365
		priority = 100
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				activate_mission = KOR_prepare_for_economy_reset
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_prepare_for_economy_reset_TT
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						KOR_economic_growth_rate_var > 2.9
					}
				}
				add_timed_idea = {
					idea = KOR_kimminseok_industrial_1_idea
					days = 365
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = debt_var_temp
					value = -40
				}
				add_debt_with_inflation = yes
			}
			else = {
				add_timed_idea = {
					idea = KOR_kimminseok_industrial_2_idea
					days = 365
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = debt_var_temp
					value = 40
				}
				add_debt_with_inflation = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				KOR_economic_growth_rate_var = 0
			}
		}
	}

	KOR_prepare_for_economy_month_growth = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_KOR_martial_dec
